the directors against their potential legal liabilities to
third parties that may be incurred in the course of
performing their duties.

by serving sufficient notice in accordance with the

convened as requested.

Our objectives are to obtain reasonable assurance           我們的目標為對綜合財務報表整體是否不存在由
about whether the consolidated financial statements as      於欺詐或錯誤而導致之重大錯誤陳述取得合理保
a whole are free from material misstatement, whether        證，並出具載列我們意見的核數師報告。我們將
due to fraud or error, and to issue an auditor’s report     此意見僅向 閣下匯報而不作其他用途。我們不
that includes our opinion. This report is made solely to    就此報告之內容，對任何其他人士負責或承擔責
you, as a body, and for no other purpose. We do not         任。
assume responsibility towards or accept liability to any
other person for the contents of this report.

Reasonable assurance is a high level of assurance, but is   合理保證屬高度保證，但不能確保按照香港審計
not a guarantee that an audit conducted in accordance       準則進行之審核總能發現某項存在之重大錯誤陳
with HKSAs will always detect a material misstatement       述。錯誤陳述可由欺詐或錯誤引起，倘合理預期
when it exists. Misstatements can arise from fraud or       有關錯誤陳述單獨或匯總起來可能影響根據該等
error and are considered material if, individually or in    綜合財務報表所作出的經濟決定，有關錯誤陳述
the aggregate, they could reasonably be expected to         即被視作重大。
influence the economic decisions of users taken on the

their costs over their estimated useful lives. The              備之估計可使用年期如下：

– Buildings and leasehold       the shorter of 5-20 years       – 樓宇及租賃物業       5至20年及租約年期，
     improvements                 and the lease term               裝修            以較短者為準

2.3     Investment properties                                   2.3      投資物業

Investment property, principally comprising leasehold           投資物業，主要由租賃樓宇組成，持有為獲得長
buildings, is held for long-term rental yields or for           期租金收益或作為資本增值或兩者兼備同時並
capital appreciation or both, and is not occupied by the        非由本集團佔用。如符合投資物業的所有定義，
Group. Land held under operating leases are accounted           營運租賃下的土地作為投資物業入賬。在此情況
for as investment properties when the rest of the               下，有關的營運租賃猶如融資租賃入賬。
definition of an investment property is met. In such
cases, the operating leases concerned are accounted for
as if they were finance leases.

Investment property is initially measured at cost,             投資物業始初按成本列賬，包括相關的交易成本
in c l u d i n g r e l a ted transac tion c osts and, wh ere   及（如適用）借貸成本。在始初確認後，投資物業
applicable, borrowing costs. After initial recognition,        按公允值列賬，公允值指由外部估值師於每個報
investment properties are carried at fair value,               告日期釐定的公開市值。公允值乃根據活躍市場
representing open market value determined at each              價格計算，並就個別資產的性質、地點或狀況作
reporting date by external valuers. Fair value is based        出必要調整。倘未能獲取該等資料，本集團採用
on active market prices, adjusted, if necessary, for           其他估值方法，例如近期較不活躍市場的價格或
any difference in the nature, location or condition of         折現現金流量預測。重建並繼續用作投資物業之
the specific asset. If this information is not available,      投資物業，或其市場活躍度下降之投資物業繼續
the Group uses alternative valuation methods such              按公允值計量。公允值變動將在綜合損益表內的
as recent prices on less active markets or discounted          「其他收入－淨額」中確認。
cash flow projections. Investment property that is
being redeveloped for continuing use as investment

continues to be measured at fair value. Changes in fair

assumptions about rental income from future leases in        公允值亦反映，在類似基準下該物業預期的任何
light of current market conditions. The fair value also      現金流出。

be expected in respect of the property.

carrying amount only when it is probable that future         濟利益將很有可能流入本集團並且能可靠地作出

reliably. All other repairs and maintenance costs are        支銷。
expensed in the consolidated statement of profit or loss
during the financial period in which they are incurred.

If an investment property becomes owner-occupied, it         倘一項投資物業成為自用物業，則須被重分類為
is reclassified as property, plant and equipment, and        物業、廠房及設備，其於重分類當日之公允值，
its fair value at the date of reclassification becomes       就會計目的而言成為其成本。現正興建或發展中
its cost for accounting purposes. Property that is           以於日後作投資物業用途的物業，被分類為在建
under construction or development for future use             投資物業。當在建投資物業之公允值未能可靠計
as investment property is classified as investment           量時，該物業會以成本值計量，直至物業落成之
property under construction. Where fair value of             日期或公允值能夠可靠計量之日期兩者之較早者
investment properties under construction is not reliably     為止。
measurable, the property is measured at cost until the
earlier of the date construction is completed or the date

investment property because its use has changed,                項物業之賬面值與於改變用途當日之公允值之差

2.7     Revenue recognition                                    2.7      收入確認

(a)     Revenue from contracts with customers                  (a)     與顧客之間之合約產生之收入

Revenue from contracts with customers is recognised            與顧客之間之合約產生之收入應於產品或服務控
when control of goods or services is transferred to the        制權轉讓至顧客時確認，其金額反映本集團預期
customers at an amount that reflects the consideration         就交換該等產品或服務而有權獲得之對價。當合
to which the Group expects to be entitled in exchange          約對價包含可變金額時，對價金額估計為本集團
for those goods or services. When the consideration            就向顧客轉讓產品或服務而有權在交換中獲取的
in a contract includes a variable amount, the amount           金額。可變對價於合約開始時估計並受到約束，
of consideration is estimated to which the Group will          直至其後關乎可變對價的不確定因素獲得解決時
be entitled in exchange for transferring the goods or          確認累計收入金額不大可能發生重大收入撥回。
services to the customer. The variable consideration is        來自轉讓產品或服務的收入於資產控制權已轉移
estimated at contract inception and constrained until it       至顧客時確認，一般情況下於顧客接收產品時，
is highly probable that a significant revenue reversal in      或倘合約訂有產品質量檢查期，於產品質量檢查
the amount of cumulative revenue recognised will not           期屆滿時。本集團認為資產的控制權於質量檢查
occur when the associated uncertainty with the variable        期結束時轉移予顧客。
consideration is subsequently resolved. Revenue from
transferring of goods or services is recognised at the
point in time when control of the asset is transferred to
the customer, generally on acceptance of the goods by
customers or where contract provides a product quality
inspection time period, the product quality inspection
time period has lapsed. The Group considers that the
control of asset is transferred to the customer at the
end of the quality inspection time period.

(b)     Contract liabilities                                   (b)     合約負債

received a consideration (or an amount of consideration        任。合約負債於本集團履行合約時確認為收入。
that is due) from the customer. Contract liabilities are
recognised as revenue when the Group performs under
the contract.

on the principal amount outstanding.

The Group’s business model for managing financial assets        本集團管理金融資產的業務模式指其如何管理其
refers to how it manages its financial assets in order to       金融資產以產生現金流量。業務模式確定現金流
generate cash flows. The business model determines              量是否來自收集合約現金流量、出售金融資產，
whether cash flows will result from collecting contractual      或兩者兼有。
cash flows, selling the financial assets, or both.

Regular way purchases and sales of financial assets are         以市場上有既定交收期買賣的金融資產於交易
recognised on trade-date, the date on which the Group           日確認，交易日為本集團承諾購買或出售資產之
commits to purchase or sell the asset. Regular way              日。市場上有既定交收期的買賣指須根據市場規
purchases or sales are purchases or sales of financial          則或慣例訂立之時間內交收資產之金融資產買
assets that require delivery of assets within the period        賣。當金融資產收取現金流量的權利屆滿或已轉
generally established by regulation or convention in the        讓且本集團已轉讓金融資產的絕大部份風險及回
market place. Financial assets are derecognised when            報時或倘本集團並無轉讓或保留金融資產的絕大
the rights to receive cash flows from the financial assets      部份風險及回報，但轉讓金融資產控制權時，終
have expired or have been transferred and either the            止確認金融資產。
Group has transferred substantially all the risks and
rewards of the financial asset or the Group has neither
transferred nor retained substantially all the risks and
rewards of the financial asset, but has transferred
control of the financial asset.

(iv)    Impairment                                              (iv)     減值

The Group recognises an allowance for expected credit           本集團就並非按公允值計入損益持有的所有債務
losses (“ECLs”) for all debt instruments not held at fair       工具確認預期信貸虧損（「預期信貸虧損」）撥備。
value through profit or loss. ECLs are based on the             預期信貸虧損乃基於根據合約到期的合約現金流
difference between the contractual cash flows due in            量與本集團預期收取並按原始實際利率的概約利
accordance with the contract and all the cash flows             率折現的所有現金流量之間的差額釐定。預期現
that the Group expects to receive, discounted at an             金流量將包括出售所持抵押品或合約條款所包含
approximation of the original effective interest rate.          的其他增信措施所得的現金流量。
The expected cash flows will include cash flows from
the sale of collateral held or other credit enhancements
that are integral to the contractual terms.

General approach                                                一般法

ECLs are recognised in two stages. For credit exposures         預期信貸虧損分兩個階段確認。就首次確認以來
for which there has not been a significant increase in          信貸風險並無大幅增加的信貸敞口而言，會為未
credit risk since initial recognition, ECLs are provided        來12個月（12個月預期信貸虧損）可能發生的違約
for credit losses that result from default events that          事件所產生的信貸虧損計提預期信貸虧損撥備。
are possible within the next 12 months (a 12 month              就首次確認以來信貸風險大幅增加的信貸敞口而
ECL). For those credit exposures for which there has            言，須就預期於敝口的餘下年期產生的信貸虧損
been a significant increase in credit risk since initial        計提虧損撥備，不論違約的時間（全期預期信 貸
recognition, a loss allowance is required for credit            虧損）。
losses expected over the remaining life of the exposure,
irrespective of the timing of the default (a lifetime ECL).

At each reporting date, the Group assesses whether              於各報告日期，本集團評估金融工具的信貸風險
the credit risk on a financial instrument has increased         自首次確認以來是否已大幅增加。於作出該評估
significantly since initial recognition. When making            時，本集團將於報告日期金融工具發生違約的風
the assessment, the Group compares the risk of a                險與於首次確認日期金融工具發生違約的風險進
default occurring on the financial instrument as at             行比較，及考慮毋須付出成本或精力即可獲得的
the reporting date with the risk of a default occurring         合理及可靠資料（包括歷史及前瞻性資料）。
on the financial instrument as at the date of initial
recognition and considers reasonable and supportable
information that is available without undue cost
or effort, including historical and forward-looking
information.

Outside basis differences                                       外在差異

Deferred tax liabilities are provided on taxable                就附屬公司、聯營公司及合營安排投資所產生之
temporary differences arising from investments in               應課稅暫時差異確認遞延所得稅負債，但不包括
subsidiaries, associates and joint arrangements, except         本集團可以控制暫時差異之撥回時間以及暫時差
for deferred tax liability where the timing of the              異在可預見將來很可能不會撥回的遞延所得稅負
reversal of the temporary difference is controlled by the       債。一般而言，本集團無法控制聯營公司之暫時
Group and it is probable that the temporary difference          差異之撥回。聯營公司之未分配溢利所產生之應
will not reverse in the foreseeable future. Generally           課稅暫時差異不予確認為遞延稅項負債，惟若有
the Group is unable to control the reversal of the              協議賦予本集團能力在可預見將來控制撥回暫時
temporary difference for associates. Only when there is         差異除外。
an agreement in place that gives the Group the ability
to control the reversal of the temporary difference in
the foreseeable future, deferred tax liability in relation
to taxable temporary differences arising from the
associate’s undistributed profits is not recognised.

Deferred tax assets are recognised on deductible                就附屬公司、聯營公司及合營安排投資產生之可
temporary differences arising from investments in               扣減暫時差異確認遞延稅項資產，但只限於暫時
subsidiaries, associates and joint arrangements only to         差異很可能於將來撥回並有充足的應課稅溢利抵
the extent that it is probable the temporary difference         銷可動用的暫時性差異。
will reverse in the future and there is sufficient taxable
profit available against which the temporary difference
can be utilised.

(iii)   Offsetting                                              (iii)    抵銷

is a legally enforceable right to offset current tax assets     負債抵銷，且遞延稅項資產和負債涉及由同一稅
against current tax liabilities and when the deferred tax       務機關向應課稅實體或不同應課稅實體徵收的所
assets and liabilities relate to income taxes levied by the     得稅，並有意按淨額結算餘款，則可將遞延稅項
same taxation authority on either the taxable entity or         資產與負債互相抵銷。
different taxable entities where there is an intention to
settle the balances on a net basis.

(k)     Provisions                                             (k)   撥備

Provisions are recognised when the Group has a                 本集團因已發生的事件而須要承擔現有的法律或
present legal or constructive obligation as a result of        推定責任；很有可能需要付出資源以償付有關責
past events; it is probable that an outflow of resources       任及金額已經可靠估計情況下確認撥備。不就未
will be required to settle the obligation; and the             來營運虧損確認撥備。
amount has been reliably estimated. Provisions are not
recognised for future operating losses.

Where there are a number of similar obligations, the           如 有 多 項 類 似 責 任，是 否 需 要 為 償 付 而 付 出 資
likelihood that an outflow of resources will be required       源，則需根據該責任類別整體考慮。即使在同一
in settlement is determined by considering the class           責任類別所包含的其中某一個項目付出資源的可
of obligations as a whole. A provision is recognised           能性極低，仍須確認撥備。
even if the likelihood of an outflow of resources with
respect to any one item included in the same class of
obligations may be small.

Provisions are measured at the present value of                撥備採用稅前利率折現預期需償付有關責任的開
expenditures expected to be required to settle the             支的現值計量，該利率反映當時市場對金錢時間
obligation using a pre-tax rate that reflects current market   值和有關責任特定風險的評估。因時間流逝而增
assessments of the time value of money and the risks           加的撥備確認為利息開支。
specific to the obligation. The increase in the provision
due to passage of time is recognised as interest expense.

(l)     Contingent liabilities                                 (l)   或有負債

A contingent liability is a possible obligation that arises    或有負債乃因已發生的事件而可能產生的責任，
from past events and whose existence will only be              並僅視乎日後會否出現一項或多項非本集團可完
confirmed by the occurrence or non-occurrence of one           全控制的不可確定事件而確認其存在。或有負債
or more uncertain future events not wholly within the          亦可因已發生的事件而產生的現有責任，但由於
control of the Group. It can also be a present obligation      未能肯定是否需要付出資源或未能可靠地估計有
arising from past events that is not recognised because it     關責任的金額而不予確認。
is not probable that outflow of resources will be required
or the amount of obligation cannot be measured reliably.

A contingent liability is not provided for as a provision      或有負債並不確認為撥備，但於財務報表附註中
but is disclosed in the notes to the financial statements.     披露。當資源流出的可能性有變而導致可能付出
When a change in the probability of an outflow of              資源，或有負債將確認為撥備。
resources occurs so that the outflow is probable, they
will then be recognised as a provision.

these exposures to ensure appropriate measures are            施。

to the fact that the Group had some business activities       活動以外幣結算。本集團之業務主要面臨就港元
denominated in foreign currencies. The Group’s                兌人民幣匯率變動產生之外匯風險。外匯風險因

Hong Kong dollars against RMB. Foreign exchange               民幣兌換外幣須遵守中國政府頒佈之外匯管制規

4.5     Impairment of non-financial assets                     4.5      非金融資產減值

The Group assesses whether there are any indicators of         本集團於財務狀況表日期評估所有非金融資產是
impairment for all non-financial assets at the statement       否有任何減值跡象。當有跡象顯示未必能收回非
of financial position date. Non-financial assets are           金融資產的賬面值時，則進行減值測試。減值存
tested for impairment when there are indicators that           在於當一項資產或一項現金產生單位的賬面值超
the carrying amounts may not be recoverable. An                逾其可收回價值時，可收回價值即其公允值減出
impairment exists when the carrying value of an asset          售成本與其使用價值的較高者。公允值減出售成
or a cash-generating unit exceeds its recoverable              本乃基於同類資產以公平基準的具約束力銷售交
amount, which is the higher of its fair value less costs       易的可得數據或可觀察市場價格減出售資產的增
of disposal and its value in use. The calculation of the       量成本計算。管理層計算使用價值時，須估計資
fair value less costs of disposal is based on available        產或現金產生單位的預計未來現金流量，選取合
data from binding sales transactions which are on              適的貼現率以計算該等現金流量的現值。
an arm’s length basis of similar assets or observable
market prices less incremental costs for disposing of the
asset. When value in use calculations are undertaken,
management has to estimate the expected future
cash flows from the asset or cash-generating unit and
choose a suitable discount rate to calculate the present
value of those cash flows.
